is_internal = False, title = None

    async def wrap_api_call(
        self, cb: Callable[[], Any], is_internal: bool = False, title: str = None
    ) -> Any:
        if self._api_zone.get():
            return await cb()
        task = asyncio.current_task(self._loop)
        parsed_st = _attach_api_call_information(
            getattr(task, "__pw_stack__", None) or _capture_stack_trace(),
            is_internal,
            title,
        )
        self._api_zone.set(parsed_st)
        try:
            return await cb()
        except Exception as error:

    async def wrap_api_call(
        self, cb: Callable[[], Any], is_internal: bool = False, title: str = None
    ) -> Any:

    def check_bulk_delete(page: Page, mod: Module, cleanup: list) -> None:
        token = unique_name("Bulk")
        for i in (1, 2):
            m_create(page, cleanup, mod, f"{token}_{i}")
        rows = find_in_list(page, token, mod.url)
        expect(rows).to_have_count(2)
        for i in range(2):
            rows.nth(i).locator("input[type='checkbox']").check()
        expect(page.get_by_text("2 selected")).to_be_visible()
        # Lua chon "Delete" chi xuat hien khi cac dong da chon xoa duoc (ban ghi chua duoc su dung)
        page.locator("select[name='bulk_actions']").select_option(label="Delete")
        page.get_by_role("button", name="Go").click()
        # Excel ky vong co hop xac nhan: cho toi da 3 giay, co thi bam Yes, khong co thi ghi nhan
        yes = page.get_by_role("button", name="Yes")
        try:
            yes.wait_for(state="visible", timeout=3_000)
            confirm_shown = True
        except PlaywrightTimeout:
            confirm_shown = False
        if confirm_shown:
            yes.click()
        expect(success_alert(page)).to_be_visible()
        find_in_list(page, token, mod.url)
        expect(no_records(page)).to_contain_text(MSG_NO_RECORDS)

    def reject(self, field):
        before = self.page.url
        self.save()
        # Success redirect proves a negative case was accepted; do not convert it to Pass.
        self.page.wait_for_function('''([url,name]) => location.href!==url ||
            [...document.querySelectorAll('.has-error,.alert-danger,.error,.invalid-feedback')]
            .some(e=>e.offsetParent!==null && e.textContent.trim()) ||
            [...document.getElementsByName(name)].some(e=>e.validity && !e.validity.valid)
        ''', arg=[before, field])
        self.shot('02_Validation')

    def assert_success(page: Page, expected: str) -> None:
        """Cho thong bao thanh cong. Neu demo reset database giua luc chay thi danh
        Skipped thay vi Failed, vi day la loi ha tang chu khong phai loi san pham."""
        for attempt in range(3):
            try:
                expect(success_alert(page)).to_contain_text(expected, timeout=10_000)
                return
            except AssertionError:
                if demo_is_resetting(page):
                    wait_for_demo(page)
                    pytest.skip("Demo reset database giua luc chay - khong ket luan duoc ket qua")

                    page.wait_for_timeout(2_000)
